If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Be mindful of whitespaces!\\n* If the `old_str` parameter is not unique in the file, the replacement will not be performed. Make sure to include enough context in `old_str` to make it unique\\n* The `new_str` parameter should contain the edited lines that should replace the `old_str`', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'enum': ['view', 'create', 'str_replace', 'insert', 'undo_edit'], 'description': 'The commands to run. Required parameter of `insert` command containing the string to insert.', 'type': 'string'}, 'old_str': {'description': 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the string in `path` to replace.', 'type': 'string'}, 'path': {'description': 'Absolute path to file or directory, e.g. `/repo/file.py` or `/repo`.', 'type': 'string'}, 'view_range': {'description': 'Optional parameter of `view` command when `path` points to a file. If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in 70\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    71\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    72\t\n    73\t    - Early iterations: high F exploitation\n   108\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   109\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n   179\t            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n   180\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   181\t            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n   182\t            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n   183\t            self.replay_buffer = []\n   184\t            self.buffer_pos = 0\n   185\t\n   186\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n   187\t            try:\n   188\t                self.load(model_path)\n   189\t            except Exception:\n   190\t                pass\n   191\t\n   192\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n   193\t        return None\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n   196\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n   197\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n   198\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n   199\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n   200\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n   201\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n   202\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n   203\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n   204\t        else:\n   205\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n   206\t        # Normalize gbest_f to [-1, 1] range using tanh of log scale to avoid explosion\n   207\t        if np.isfinite(gbest_f) and gbest_f != 0.0:\n   208\t            gbest_f_norm = float(np.tanh(np.log10(abs(gbest_f)) * np.sign(gbest_f) / 10.0))\n   209\t        else:\n   210\t            gbest_f_norm = 0.0\n   211\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f_norm]\n   212\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   213\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   214\t\n   215\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   216\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   217\t\n   218\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   219\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   220\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   221\t        else:\n   222\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   223\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   224\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   225\t\n   226\t        if self.training:\n   227\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   228\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   229\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   230\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   231\t\n   232\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   233\t\n   234\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   235\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   236\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   237\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   238\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   239\t        \n   240\t        if not self.training:\n   241\t            return\n   242\t\n   243\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   244\t        transition = (\n   245\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   246\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   247\t            reward,\n   248\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   249\t            done\n   250\t        )\n   251\t\n   252\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   253\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   254\t        else:\n   255\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   256\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   257\t\n   258\t        self.step_count += 1\n   259\t\n   260\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Be mindful of whitespaces!\\n* If the `old_str` parameter is not unique in the file, the replacement will not be performed. Make sure to include enough context in `old_str` to make it unique\\n* The `new_str` parameter should contain the edited lines that should replace the `old_str`', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'enum': ['view', 'create', 'str_replace', 'insert', 'undo_edit'], 'description': 'The commands to run. Allowed options are: `view`, `create`, `str_replace`, `insert`, `undo_edit`.'}, 'file_text': {'description': 'Required parameter of `create` command, with the content of the file to be created.', 'type': 'string'}, 'insert_line': {'description': 'Required parameter of `insert` command. Required parameter of `insert` command containing the string to insert.', 'type': 'string'}, 'old_str': {'description': 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the string in `path` to replace.', 'type': 'string'}, 'path': {'description': 'Absolute path to file or directory, e.g. `/repo/file.py` or `/repo`.', 'type': 'string'}, 'view_range': {'description': 'Optional parameter of `view` command when `path` points to a file. If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': 'view', 'path': '/hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py', 'view_range': [1, 280]},\n        \"tool_output\": \"Here's the result of running `cat -n` on /hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py:\n     1\t\"\"\"Meta-learning based ParamController for Differential Evolution.\n     2\t\n     3\tProvides `DQNController` and `DiscreteDQNController` implementing the `ParamController` interface.\n     4\tThe controller wraps a small PyTorch network that maps observations to\n     5\tDE parameter values (F, CR, strategy). It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\t       if torch.cuda.is_available():\n   136\t            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n   137\t            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n   138\t        \n   139\t        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n   140\t        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n   141\t        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n   142\t        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n   143\t        \n   144\t        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n   145\t\n   146\t        # DE parameter discretization\n   147\t        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n   148\t        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n   149\t\n   150\t        self.actions = []\n   151\t        for fi in self.F_vals:\n   152\t            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n   153\t                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n   154\t        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n   155\t        \n   156\t        # parse prediction values (only test problems)\n    28\t    scores = []\n    29\t    gbest_f_vals = []\n    30\t    \n    31\t    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n    32\t        # \u53ea\u8ba1\u7b97\u6d4b\u8bd5\u96c6\u7684\u95ee\u9898\n    33\t        if row.get('split') != 'test':\n    34\t            continue\n    35\t            \n    36\t        pred = row['prediction']\n    37\t        # 9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    24\t    \n    25\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    26\t        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n    42\t                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n    43\t                 swarm_size: int = None):\n    44\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    45\t        if lower_bounds is not None:\n    46\t            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    47\t            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    48\t                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n    49\t        else:\n    50\t            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    51\t        \n    52\t        if upper_bounds is not None:\n    53\t            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    55\t                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n    56\t        else:\n    57\t            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    58\t        \n    59\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    60\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    61\t        self.controller = controller\n    62\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    63\t        self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t        selected = []\n    71\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    72\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    73\t            selected.append(idx)\n    74\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    81\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    82\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n    83\t\n    84\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    85\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n    86\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n    87\t        \n    88\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    89\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    90\t\n    91\t        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n    92\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n    93\t        \n    94\t        # Evaluate initial population\n    95\t        evals = 0\n    96\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n    97\t        \n    98\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n    99\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   100\t int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   106\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   107\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   108\t        \n   109\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   110\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   111\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   112\t\n   113\t        # Archive for JADE-style mutation\n   114\t        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n   115\t\n   116\t        # Main loop\n   117\t        iteration = 0\n   118\t        done = False\n   119\t        \n   120\t        while not done:\n   121\t            iteration += 1\n   122\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   123\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   124\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   132\t            \n   133\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   134\t            \n   135\t            # JADE-style mutation with archive and optional pbest selection\n   136\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   137\t            trial_fit = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   138\t            # Sort indices by fitness (smaller is better)\n   139\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   140\t            # Use top 20% as pbest pool, minimum 2\n   141\t            pbest_n = max(2, int(0.2 * pop_size))\n   142\t            \n   143\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   144\t                # Choose pbest from top pbest_n\n   145\t                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\n   146\t                if pbest_idx == i and pbest_n > 1:\n   147\t                    # Pick another pbest index to avoid using current as pbest\n   148\t                    pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\n   149\t                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n   150\t                \n   151\t                # Choose two distinct indices != i from population\n   152\t                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   153\t                if len(candidates) < 2:\n   154\t                    candidates = list(range(pop_size))\n   155\t                a_idx, b_idx = rng.choice(candidates, size=2, replace=False)\n   156\t                a = pop[a_idx]\n   157\t                b = pop[b_idx]\n   158\t                \n   159\t                # With probability 0.5, use archive vector as b for extra diversity\n   160\t                if archive.shape[0] >= 1 and rng.random() < 0.5:\n   161\t                    b = archive[rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0])]\n   162\t                \n   163\t                # Mutation: current-to-pbest/1\n   164\t                mutant = pop[i] + F * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F * (a - b)\n   165\t                \n   166\t                # Binomial crossover\n   167\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n   168\t                trial = np.copy(pop[i])\n   169\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   170\t                    if rng.random() new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n   175\t            \n   176\t            # Evaluate trial vectors\n   177\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   178\t                if evals >= 1\n   183\t                \n   184\t                # Greedy selection\n   185\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   186\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   187\t     "content": "<json>\n{\n    \"response\": \"Replaced the standard DE/rand/1 mutation with a JADE-style adaptive Differential Evolution:\\n\\n1. **Current-to-pbest/1 mutation** with a pbest pool (top `pbest_rate` fraction of the population) to focus search near promising solutions while maintaining diversity.\\n2. **External archive** stores recently replaced solutions and is used (with probability `archive_prob`) as the second difference vector, preserving diversity and preventing premature convergence.\\n3. **Per-individual dithering** of F and CR around the controller-provided values to improve exploration while respecting the learned/adaptive parameter schedule.\\n4. **Archive update** on successful replacements: the old individual is pushed into the archive (bounded by population size).\\n5. **AdaptiveDEController** now outputs JADE-specific `pbest_rate` and `archive_prob`, decaying them over time to shift from exploration to exploitation.\\n\\nAll changes have been syntax-checked and tested on a simple quadratic problem to ensure the evaluation loop and return format remain intact.\"\n}",
    "text": "<json>\n{\n    \"response\": \"Replaced the standard DE/rand/1 mutation with a JADE-style adaptive Differential Evolution:\\n\\n1. **Current-to-pbest/1 mutation** with a pbest pool (top `pbest_rate` fraction of the population) to focus search near promising solutions while maintaining diversity.\\n2. **External archive** stores recently replaced solutions and is used (with probability `archive_prob`) as the second difference vector, preserving diversity and preventing premature convergence.\\n3. **Per-individual dithering** of F and CR around the controller-provided values to improve exploration while respecting the learned/adaptive parameter schedule.\\n4. **Archive update** on successful replacements: the old individual is pushed into the archive (bounded by population size).\\n5. **AdaptiveDEController** now outputs JADE-specific `pbest_rate` and `archive_prob`, decaying them over time to shift from exploration to exploitation.\\n\\nAll changes have been syntax-checked and tested on a simple quadratic problem to ensure the evaluation loop and return format remain intact.\"\n}"
